[build_npz] build_mars_training_npz
Reason: Build MARS train/validation full-video windows for the final YOLO/SPPF classifier matrix.
Command:
python <RUN_ROOT>\shared_scripts\scripts\prepare_full_video_npz.py --train_splits train --val_splits validation --exclude_splits test_1 test_2 --mars_root <MARS_DATA_ROOT> --yolo_weights <RUN_ROOT>\MARS_YOLO_Pose_Model\weights\best.pt --output_root <RUN_ROOT>\outputs\npz_cache\mars_full_video --source_mode mp4 --source_mp4_cache <RUN_ROOT>\outputs\source_mp4_cache\mars --window_size 32 --window_stride 16 --crop_size 224 --group_crop_size 224 --animal_scale_factor 4.0 --group_scale_factor 8.0 --pose_conf_threshold 0.2 --yolo_batch 16 --npz_writers 1 --other_subsample 0.3 --seed 42 --device cuda:0 --skip_existing
Expected outputs:
  - <RUN_ROOT>\outputs\npz_cache\mars_full_video\sequence_manifest.json
